country_event = {
    id = 33337
    title = "serf_cleanup"
    desc = "EVTDESC33337"
    #picture = "danishgovernment"

    is_triggered_only = yes

    trigger = {
        ai = yes
        exists = yes
        war = no
        NOT = { has_country_modifier = law_changed_by_force }
        OR = {
            AND = {
                NOT = { debt_law = serfdom }
				has_country_flag = serfdom_abolished
                any_owned_province = { has_pop_type = serfs }
            }
            any_neighbor_country = {
                ai = no
                exists = yes
                NOT = { debt_law = serfdom }
				has_country_flag = serfdom_abolished
                any_owned_province = { has_pop_type = serfs }
            }
            any_greater_power = {
                ai = no
                NOT = { debt_law = serfdom }
				has_country_flag = serfdom_abolished
                any_owned_province = { has_pop_type = serfs }
            }
        }
    }

    allow_multiple_instances = no

    option = {
        name = "EVT33337OPTA"
        add_country_modifier = { name = law_changed_by_force duration = 365 }
            random_owned = {
                limit = {
                    owner = {
                        any_owned_province = { has_pop_type = serfs }
						has_country_flag = serfdom_abolished
                        NOT = { debt_law = serfdom }
                    }
                }
                owner = {
                    any_owned = {
                        limit = {
                            has_pop_type = serfs
                            has_pop_type = labourers
                        }
                        any_pop = { limit = { type = serfs } pop_type = labourers }
                    }
                    any_owned = {
                        limit = {
                            has_pop_type = serfs
                            has_pop_type = farmers
                        }
                        any_pop = { limit = { type = serfs } pop_type = farmers }
                    }
                }
            }
            any_country = {
                limit = {
                    exists = yes
                    any_owned_province = { has_pop_type = serfs }
					has_country_flag = serfdom_abolished
                    NOT = { debt_law = serfdom }
                }
                any_owned = {
                    limit = {
                        has_pop_type = serfs
                        has_pop_type = labourers
                    }
                    any_pop = { limit = { type = serfs } pop_type = labourers }
                }
                any_owned = {
                    limit = {
                        has_pop_type = serfs
                        has_pop_type = farmers
                    }
                    any_pop = { limit = { type = serfs } pop_type = farmers }
                }
            }
			
		
		
		
    }
}



country_event = {
    id = 33338
    title = "thrallcleanup"
    desc = "EVTDESC33337"
    #picture = "danishgovernment"

    is_triggered_only = yes

    trigger = {
        ai = yes
        exists = yes
        war = no
        NOT = { has_country_modifier = law_changed_by_force }
        OR = {
            AND = {
                NOT = { thralldom = thralldom_allowed }
                any_owned_province = { has_pop_type = thralls }
            }
            any_neighbor_country = {
                ai = no
                exists = yes
                NOT = { thralldom = thralldom_allowed }
                any_owned_province = { has_pop_type = thralls }
            }
            any_greater_power = {
                ai = no
                NOT = { thralldom = thralldom_allowed }
                any_owned_province = { has_pop_type = thralls }
            }
        }
    }

    allow_multiple_instances = no

    option = {
        name = "EVT33338OPTA"
        add_country_modifier = { name = law_changed_by_force duration = 365 }
            random_owned = {
                limit = {
                    owner = {
                        any_owned_province = { has_pop_type = thralls }
                        NOT = { thralldom = thralldom_allowed }
                    }
                }
                owner = {
                    any_owned = {
                        limit = {
                            has_pop_type = thralls
                            has_pop_type = labourers
                        }
                        any_pop = { limit = { type = thralls } pop_type = labourers }
                    }
                    any_owned = {
                        limit = {
                            has_pop_type = thralls
                            has_pop_type = farmers
                        }
                        any_pop = { limit = { type = thralls } pop_type = farmers }
                    }
                }
            }
            any_country = {
                limit = {
                    exists = yes
                    any_owned_province = { has_pop_type = thralls }
                    NOT = { thralldom = thralldom_allowed }
                }
                any_owned = {
                    limit = {
                        has_pop_type = thralls
                        has_pop_type = labourers
                    }
                    any_pop = { limit = { type = thralls } pop_type = labourers }
                }
                any_owned = {
                    limit = {
                        has_pop_type = thralls
                        has_pop_type = farmers
                    }
                    any_pop = { limit = { type = thralls } pop_type = farmers }
                }
            }
    }
}

country_event = {
    id = 50238
    title = "fed_flags"
    desc = "EVTDESC33337"
    #picture = "danishgovernment"

    is_triggered_only = yes

    trigger = {
        ai = yes
        exists = yes
        war = no
        NOT = { has_country_modifier = law_changed_by_force }
       OR = {
			AND = {
				legislative_system = tribal_assembly
				NOT = { has_country_flag = federalised }
				civilized = yes
			}
           any_neighbor_country = {
               ai = no
               legislative_system = tribal_assembly
				NOT = { has_country_flag = federalised }
				civilized = yes
           }
           any_greater_power = {
               ai = no
               legislative_system = tribal_assembly
				NOT = { has_country_flag = federalised }
				civilized = yes
           }
        }
    }

    allow_multiple_instances = no

    option = {
        name = "EVT33337OPTA"
        add_country_modifier = { name = law_changed_by_force duration = 365 }
            random_owned = {
                limit = {
                    owner = {
                        legislative_system = tribal_assembly
						civilized = yes
                    }
                }
                owner = {
                    set_country_flag = federalised
                }
            }
            any_country = {
                limit = {
                    exists = yes
                    legislative_system = tribal_assembly
					NOT = { has_country_flag = federalised }
					civilized = yes
                }
                set_country_flag = federalised
            }
			
		
		
		
    }
}



country_event = {
    id = 50290
    title = "usufruct_flags"
    desc = "EVTDESC33337"
    #picture = "danishgovernment"

    is_triggered_only = yes

    trigger = {
        ai = yes
        exists = yes
        war = no
        NOT = { has_country_modifier = law_changed_by_force }
        OR = {
			AND = {
				land_reform_civ = usufruct_prioritised
				NOT = { has_country_flag = usufruct_law_country }
				civilized = yes
			}
           any_neighbor_country = {
               ai = no
				land_reform_civ = usufruct_prioritised
				NOT = { has_country_flag = usufruct_law_country }
				civilized = yes
           }
           any_greater_power = {
               ai = no
               land_reform_civ = usufruct_prioritised
				NOT = { has_country_flag = usufruct_law_country }
				civilized = yes
           }
        }
    }

    allow_multiple_instances = no

    option = {
        name = "EVT33337OPTA"
        add_country_modifier = { name = law_changed_by_force duration = 365 }
            random_owned = {
                limit = {
                    owner = {
                        land_reform_civ = usufruct_prioritised
						NOT = { has_country_flag = usufruct_law_country }
						civilized = yes
                    }
                }
                owner = {
                    set_country_flag = usufruct_law_country
                }
            }
            any_country = {
                limit = {
                    exists = yes
                    land_reform_civ = usufruct_prioritised
					NOT = { has_country_flag = usufruct_law_country }
					civilized = yes
                }
                set_country_flag = usufruct_law_country
            }
			
		
		
		
    }
}

country_event = {
    id = 50240
    title = "fed_swaper"
    desc = "EVTDESC33337"
    #picture = "danishgovernment"

    is_triggered_only = yes

    trigger = {
        ai = yes
        exists = yes
        war = no
        NOT = { has_country_modifier = law_changed_by_force }
        OR = {
			AND = {
				has_country_flag = federalised
				OR = {
					legislative_system = noble_parliament
					legislative_system = regulated_elections
					legislative_system = approved_candidacy 
					legislative_system = appointed_legislatures
					
				}
			}
            any_neighbor_country = {
                ai = no
                has_country_flag = federalised
				OR = {
					legislative_system = noble_parliament
					legislative_system = regulated_elections
					legislative_system = approved_candidacy 
					legislative_system = appointed_legislatures
					
				}
            }
            any_greater_power = {
                ai = no
                has_country_flag = federalised
				OR = {
					legislative_system = noble_parliament
					legislative_system = regulated_elections
					legislative_system = approved_candidacy 
					legislative_system = appointed_legislatures
					
				}
            }
        }
    }

    allow_multiple_instances = no

    option = {
        name = "EVT33337OPTA"
        add_country_modifier = { name = law_changed_by_force duration = 365 }
            random_owned = {
                limit = {
                    owner = {
                        has_country_flag = federalised
						OR = {
							legislative_system = noble_parliament
							legislative_system = regulated_elections
							legislative_system = approved_candidacy 
							legislative_system = appointed_legislatures
							
						}
                    }
                }
                owner = {
					political_reform = centralised
                    set_country_flag = centralised
					clr_country_flag = federalised
                }
            }
            any_country = {
                limit = {
                    exists = yes
                    has_country_flag = federalised
					OR = {
						legislative_system = noble_parliament
						legislative_system = regulated_elections
						legislative_system = approved_candidacy 
						legislative_system = appointed_legislatures
							
					}
                }
                political_reform = centralised
                set_country_flag = centralised
				clr_country_flag = federalised
            }
			
		
		
		
    }
}

country_event = {
    id = 50292
    title = "usufruct_swaper"
    desc = "EVTDESC33337"
    #picture = "danishgovernment"

    is_triggered_only = yes

    trigger = {
        ai = yes
        exists = yes
        war = no
        NOT = { has_country_modifier = law_changed_by_force }
        OR = {
			AND = {
				has_country_flag = usufruct_law_country
				OR = {
					land_reform_civ = feudal_tenure
					land_reform_civ = enclosure_of_commons
					land_reform_civ = private_landownership
					land_reform_civ = farm_cooperatives
					land_reform_civ = collectivized_farming
					
				}
			}
            any_neighbor_country = {
                ai = no
                has_country_flag = usufruct_law_country
				OR = {
					land_reform_civ = feudal_tenure
					land_reform_civ = enclosure_of_commons
					land_reform_civ = private_landownership
					land_reform_civ = farm_cooperatives
					land_reform_civ = collectivized_farming
					
				}
            }
            any_greater_power = {
                ai = no
                has_country_flag = usufruct_law_country
				OR = {
					land_reform_civ = feudal_tenure
					land_reform_civ = enclosure_of_commons
					land_reform_civ = private_landownership
					land_reform_civ = farm_cooperatives
					land_reform_civ = collectivized_farming
					
				}
            }
        }
    }

    allow_multiple_instances = no

    option = {
        name = "EVT33337OPTA"
        add_country_modifier = { name = law_changed_by_force duration = 365 }
            random_owned = {
                limit = {
                    owner = {
                        has_country_flag = usufruct_law_country
						OR = {
							land_reform_civ = feudal_tenure
							land_reform_civ = enclosure_of_commons
							land_reform_civ = private_landownership
							land_reform_civ = farm_cooperatives
							land_reform_civ = collectivized_farming
							
						}
                    }
                }
                owner = {
					social_reform = realty_prioritised
                    set_country_flag = realty_law_country
					clr_country_flag = usufruct_law_country
                }
            }
            any_country = {
                limit = {
                    exists = yes
                    has_country_flag = usufruct_law_country
					OR = {
						land_reform_civ = feudal_tenure
						land_reform_civ = enclosure_of_commons
						land_reform_civ = private_landownership
						land_reform_civ = farm_cooperatives
						land_reform_civ = collectivized_farming
						
					}
                }
                social_reform = realty_prioritised
                set_country_flag = realty_law_country
				clr_country_flag = usufruct_law_country
            }
			
		
		
		
    }
}

country_event = {
    id = 50242
    title = "federalisation_locking"
    desc = "EVTDESC33337"
    #picture = "danishgovernment"

    is_triggered_only = yes

    trigger = {
        ai = yes
        exists = yes
        war = no
        NOT = { has_country_modifier = law_changed_by_force }
        OR = {
			AND = {
				OR = {
					federalisation = states_system
					federalisation = vocational_candidacy
				}
				NOT = { has_country_flag = locked_fed }
			}
            any_neighbor_country = {
                ai = no
                OR = {
					federalisation = states_system
					federalisation = vocational_candidacy
				}
				NOT = { has_country_flag = locked_fed }
            }
            any_greater_power = {
                ai = no
                OR = {
					federalisation = states_system
					federalisation = vocational_candidacy
				}
				NOT = { has_country_flag = locked_fed }
            }
        }
    }

    allow_multiple_instances = no

    option = {
        name = "EVT33337OPTA"
        add_country_modifier = { name = law_changed_by_force duration = 365 }
            random_owned = {
                limit = {
                    owner = {
						OR = {
							federalisation = states_system
							federalisation = vocational_candidacy
						}
                    }
                }
                owner = {
                    set_country_flag = locked_fed
                }
            }
            any_country = {
                limit = {
                    exists = yes
                    OR = {
						federalisation = states_system
						federalisation = vocational_candidacy
					}
					NOT = { has_country_flag = locked_fed }
                }
                set_country_flag = locked_fed
            }
			
		
		
		
    }
}

    id = 50409

    desc = "EVTDESC33337"
    #picture = "danishgovernment"

    is_triggered_only = yes

    trigger = {
        ai = yes
        exists = yes

        NOT = { has_country_modifier = law_changed_by_force }

				}
				has_country_flag = locked_fed
			}
            any_neighbor_country = {
                ai = no
                NOT = {
					federalisation = states_system
					federalisation = vocational_candidacy
				}
				has_country_flag = locked_fed
            }
            any_greater_power = {
                ai = no
                NOT = {
					federalisation = states_system
					federalisation = vocational_candidacy
				}
				has_country_flag = locked_fed
            }
        }

    id = 50364

    desc = "EVTDESC33337"
    #picture = "danishgovernment"

	is_triggered_only = yes

    trigger = {
        ai = yes
        exists = yes
        war = no
        
        OR = {
			any_owned_province = {
                    state_scope = { 
						flashpoint_tension = 5 
						any_owned_province = {
							is_core = BUD
						}
						NOT = {
							any_owned_province = {
								OR = {
									port = yes
									any_neighbor_province = {
										owner = { 
											tag = BUD
										}
									}
								}
							}
						}
					}
                    owner = {
                        tag = RAN
                    }
					
                }
			any_owned_province = {
                    state_scope = { flashpoint_tension = 5 }
                    owner = {
                        OR = {
                            NOT = { has_global_flag = crisis_enabled }
                            is_vassal = yes
                        }
                    }
                }
            any_owned_province = {
                any_neighbor_province = {
                    empty = yes
                    state_scope = { flashpoint_tension = 5 }
                }
                owner = { NOT = { has_global_flag = crisis_enabled } }
            }
            any_greater_power = {
                ai = no
				OR = {
					AND = {
						NOT = { has_global_flag = crisis_enabled }
						any_owned_province = {
							state_scope = { flashpoint_tension = 5 }
						}
					}
					AND = {
						tag = RAN
						any_owned_province = {
							state_scope = { 
								flashpoint_tension = 5 
								any_owned_province = {
									is_core = BUD
								}
								NOT = {
									any_owned_province = {
										OR = {
											port = yes
											any_neighbor_province = {
												owner = { 
													tag = BUD
												}
											}
										}
									}

country_event = {
    id = 50280
    title = "medicine_flags"
    desc = "EVTDESC33337"
    #picture = "danishgovernment"

	is_triggered_only = yes

    trigger = {
        ai = yes
        exists = yes
        war = no
        
        OR = {
			OR = {
				AND = {
					NOT = { has_country_flag = discovered_vaccines }
					invention = vaccination
				}
			
				AND = {
					NOT = { has_country_flag = discovered_germs }
					invention = germ_theory
				
				}
				
				AND = {
					NOT = { has_country_flag = discovered_lighting }
					invention = gaslights
				
				}
				
				AND = {
					NOT = { has_country_flag = discovered_xrays }
					invention = x_rays
				
				}
			
			}
			
            any_greater_power = {
                OR = {
					AND = {
						NOT = { has_country_flag = discovered_vaccines }
						invention = vaccination
					}
				
					AND = {
						NOT = { has_country_flag = discovered_germs }
						invention = germ_theory
					
					}
					
					AND = {
						NOT = { has_country_flag = discovered_lighting }
						invention = gaslights
					
					}
					
					AND = {
						NOT = { has_country_flag = discovered_xrays }
						invention = x_rays
					
					}
			
				}
            }
            any_neighbor_country = {
                OR = {
					AND = {
						NOT = { has_country_flag = discovered_vaccines }
						invention = vaccination
					}
				
					AND = {
						NOT = { has_country_flag = discovered_germs }
						invention = germ_theory
					
					}
					
					AND = {
						NOT = { has_country_flag = discovered_lighting }
						invention = gaslights
					
					}
					
					AND = {
						NOT = { has_country_flag = discovered_xrays }

country_event = {
	id = 246246
	title = "EVTNAME246246"
	desc = "EVTDESC246246"
	

    trigger = {
        ai = no
        number_of_states = 2
        any_owned_province = {
			is_colonial = no
		}
        OR = {
            war = yes
            NOT = {
               total_amount_of_divisions = 5
            }
        }
        OR = {
            any_owned_province = {
                any_core = {
                    neighbor = THIS
                    is_independant = yes
                    civilized = yes
                    ai = yes
		            war = no
		            total_amount_of_divisions = 5
                    NOT = {
                        alliance_with = THIS
                        war_exhaustion = 30
                        relation = { who = THIS value = 100 }
                    }
                }
            }
            any_neighbor_country = {
                is_independant = yes
                civilized = yes
                ai = yes
		        war = no
		        total_amount_of_divisions = 5
                NOT = {
                    alliance_with = THIS
                    war_exhaustion = 30
                    relation = { who = THIS value = -50 }
                }
            }

        }
        
	}
	
	mean_time_to_happen = {
		months = 360 #180

	}

    option = {
		name = "This does not bode well..."
		random_country = {
            limit = {
                any_owned_province = {
                    any_neighbor_province = {
                        owned_by = THIS
                    }
                }
                is_independant = yes
                civilized = yes
                ai = yes
		        war = no
		        total_amount_of_divisions = 5
                NOT = {
                 alliance_with = THIS
                    war_exhaustion = 30
                 relation = { who = THIS value = 100 }
                }
            }
               country_event = {
	                id = 123123
	                days = 5
                }
        }
        
	}

}

# AI_WAR_OF_OPPERTUNITY
country_event = {
	id = 123123
	title = "EVTNAME123123"
	desc = "EVTDESC123123"
	
	is_triggered_only = yes

   
	
	option = {
		name = "EVT123123OPTA"

         if = {
        limit = {
            FROM = {
                any_owned_province = {
                    is_core = THIS
                }
            }
        }

        FROM = {
			add_casus_belli = {
				target = THIS
				type = acquire_all_cores
				months = 12
			}
		}
		
		THIS = {
			war = {
				target = FROM
				attacker_goal = { casus_belli = acquire_all_cores }
				call_ally = yes
			}
            set_country_flag = cb_check_done
		}

    }

        if = {
            limit = {
                THIS = {
                    NOT = {
                        has_country_flag = cb_check_done
                    }
                }

            }
		    FROM = {
			    add_casus_belli = {
				    target = THIS
				    type = cut_down_to_size
				    months = 12
			    }
		    }
		
		    THIS = {
			    war = {
				    target = FROM
				    attacker_goal = { casus_belli = cut_down_to_size }
				    call_ally = yes
			    }
		    }
        }
        clr_country_flag = cb_check_done
	}
}

country_event = {
	id = 123124
	title = "EVTNAME123124"
	desc = "EVTDESC123124"
	
	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	allow_multiple_instances = no

	trigger = {
		ai = yes
		exists = yes
		OR = {
			AND = {
				has_country_flag = lost_containment_war
			}
			any_neighbor_country = {
				ai = no
				exists = yes
				has_country_flag = lost_containment_war
			}
			any_greater_power = {
				ai = no
				has_country_flag = lost_containment_war
			}
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		name = "containment_cleanup_fired"
		if = {
			limit = {
				THIS = {
					check_variable = {
						which =  international_hegemon
						value = 1
					}
					has_country_flag = lost_containment_war
				}
			}
			THIS = {
				change_variable = {
					which = international_hegemon
					value = -1
				}
				clr_country_flag = lost_containment_war
			}
		}
		
		any_country = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = {
					which =  international_hegemon
					value = 1
				}
				has_country_flag = lost_containment_war
			}
			change_variable = {
				which = international_hegemon
				value = -1
			}
			clr_country_flag = lost_containment_war
			
		}
	}

}

country_event = {
	id = 123125
	title = "EVTNAME123125"
	desc = "EVTDESC123125"
	
	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	allow_multiple_instances = no

	trigger = {
		ai = yes
		exists = yes
		OR = {
			AND = {
				has_country_flag = won_hegemony_war
			}
			any_neighbor_country = {
				ai = no
				exists = yes
				has_country_flag = won_hegemony_war
			}
			any_greater_power = {
				ai = no
				has_country_flag = won_hegemony_war
			}
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		name = "containment_cleanup_fired_2"
		if = {
			limit = {
				THIS = {
					check_variable = {
						which =  international_hegemon
						value = 0
					}
					has_country_flag = won_hegemony_war
				}
			}
			THIS = {
				change_variable = {
					which = international_hegemon
					value = 1
				}
				clr_country_flag = won_hegemony_war
			}
		}
		
		if = {
			limit = {
				THIS = {
					NOT = { 
						check_variable = {
							which =  international_hegemon
							value = 0
						}
					}
					has_country_flag = won_hegemony_war
				}
			}
			THIS = {
				set_variable = {
					which = international_hegemon
					value = 1
				}
				clr_country_flag = won_hegemony_war
			}
		}
		
		any_country = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = {
					which =  international_hegemon
					value = 0
				}
				has_country_flag = won_hegemony_war
			}
			change_variable = {
				which = international_hegemon
				value = 1
			}
			clr_country_flag = won_hegemony_war
			
		}
		
		any_country = {
			limit = {
				NOT = {
					check_variable = {
						which =  international_hegemon
						value = 0
					}
				}
				has_country_flag = won_hegemony_war
			}
			set_variable = {
				which = international_hegemon
				value = 1
			}
			clr_country_flag = won_hegemony_war
			
		}
	}

}
